//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") || !options.flags.contains_key("residency") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&!options.flags.contains_key("residency") => {
            snapback4_core::Store::memory().and_then(|store|snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),fixture_now)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
        },
        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    };
    let mut engine = match opened {
        Ok(engine) => engine,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    };

            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }

            }
            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
                return 1;
            }
            return 0;
        }

    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&populated_fixture{
        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
        }
    }else{
        snapback4_lang::security::challenges::generated_campaign(&bound,&mut security_campaign,true);
    }
    let security_campaign=snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run_selected_after(&bound,None,security_campaign);
    let mut security_campaign=security_campaign;
    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
    if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {if let Err(why)=security::remember(&project,options,&security_campaign,security_http_ok){eprintln!("security: campaign record unavailable: {why}; check cannot reuse these refutations");}}
    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);

    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
    let reachable=security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).count();
    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {reachable} reachable violations; {} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing); finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len()-reachable);
    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability.is_reachable()) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}

    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    }

    let mut failures = 0;
    println!("test: {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "operation (persona)", "examined", "entries", "probes", "steps", "heap B", "output B");
    println!("      {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "ceilings", ceilings.examined, ceilings.index_entries, ceilings.rule_probes, ceilings.steps, ceilings.heap_bytes, ceilings.output_bytes);
    println!("legend: examined=rows visited; entries=index visits; probes=rule lookups; steps=interpreter work; heap B=charged heap; output B=encoded answer.");
    println!("verdicts: complete=whole answer; capped=partial; refused=expected-case or program-require refusal; denied=unexpected refusal; not run=no coverage; case OK=expectation passed.");
    let mut skipped = Vec::new();
    let mut held_not_run = 0;
    let mut near = Vec::new();
    let (mut complete, mut capped, mut refused_derived, mut expected) = (0usize, 0usize, 0usize, 0usize);
    let explicit = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")).map(|fixture| test_cases::explicit(fixture, &bound, fixture_now)).unwrap_or_default();
    for program in &bound.programs {
        if program.program.kind != ProgramKind::Query {
            continue;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a server fixture is not the device's held partition.
        if bound.schema.operations.get(&program.program.name).is_some_and(|fact| fact.held) {
            println!("      not run: {} is held (device only)", program.program.name);
            held_not_run += 1;
            continue;
        }
        let selected = if options.flags.contains_key("as") { &personas }
            else { sweep_personas.get(&program.program.name).unwrap_or(&personas) };
        for persona in selected {

            let name = &program.program.name;
            let cases: Vec<&test_cases::Explicit> = explicit.iter().filter(|case| case.op == *name && case.principal == principal).collect();
            // Arguments: the persona's own case for this query when it has
            // one (the builder's choice, run as written); otherwise derived
            // from the data: a reference is a row of its table, preferring
            // one whose answer is not empty (the first row is often another
            // persona's, hidden), a principal is the persona, a text is the
            // shortest that fits. What cannot be derived is named, never
            // skipped silently.
            // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — wave 7: derived arguments tested nothing when the first row was hidden from the persona.
            let mut candidates: Vec<(BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>)> = Vec::new();
            if let Some(case) = cases.iter().find(|case| case.args.is_some()) {
                let decoded: Option<BTreeMap<_, _>> = case.args.as_ref().and_then(Json::as_object).and_then(|fields| fields.iter().map(|(arg, value)| {
                    let kind = program.program.args.get(arg)?;
                    wire::from_json(kind, value).ok().map(|value| (arg.clone(), value))
                }).collect());
                if let Some(args) = decoded { candidates.push((args, Some(case.at.as_str()))); }
            }
            let mut missing = Vec::new();
            if candidates.is_empty() {
                let rows = program.program.args.values().filter_map(|kind| match kind { ir::ArgType::Ref { table } => engine.store().rows(table).ok().map(|rows| rows.len()), _ => None }).max().unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, DERIVED_ROWS);
                for at in 0..rows {
                    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
                    for (arg, kind) in &program.program.args {
                        match sample_arg(&engine, kind, &principal, at) {
                            Some(value) => { args.insert(arg.clone(), value); }
                            None if at == 0 => missing.push(format!("{arg}: {}", arg_word(kind))),
                            None => {}
                        }
                    }
                    if !missing.is_empty() { break; }
                    candidates.push((args, None));
                }
            }
            if !missing.is_empty() {
                skipped.push(format!("{name} ({persona}): needs {}", missing.join(", ")));
                continue;
            }
            // The first answer with rows wins; else the first answer; else the first refusal.
            let mut chosen: Option<(usize, BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>, Result<snapback4_core::QueryOutcome, snapback4_core::Refusal>, f64)> = None;
            let tried = candidates.len();
            for (args, source) in candidates {
                let started = Instant::now();
                let result = engine.query(name, Context::new(principal.clone(), args.clone()).at(fixture_now));
                let ms = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1000.0;
                let rank = match &result { Ok(outcome) if !empty_answer(&outcome.data) => 0, Ok(_) => 1, Err(_) => 2 };
                if chosen.as_ref().is_none_or(|(best, ..)| rank < *best) { chosen = Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)); }
                if rank == 0 || source.is_some() { break; }
            }
            let Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)) = chosen else { continue };
            let shown = if args.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" {}", serde_json::to_string(&args.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), wire::to_json(v))).collect::<serde_json::Map<_, _>>()).unwrap_or_default()) };
            let arguments = shown.clone();
            let shown = match source { Some(at) => format!("{shown} (from {at})"), None => shown };
            let derived = !args.is_empty() && source.is_none();
            let label = format!("{name} ({persona})");
            match result {
                Ok(outcome) => {
                    let m = outcome.metered;
                    let state = if outcome.complete { "complete" } else { "capped" };
                    if outcome.complete { complete += 1 } else { capped += 1 }
                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22.1 — report approach to a ceiling on the fixture, separately from actual cuts.
                    if outcome.capped_at.is_none() && outcome.word_cut_at.is_none() {
                        if let Some(cost) = crate::serve::read_cost::near_ceiling(m, ceilings) { near.push(format!("{label} {cost}")); }
                    }
                    let empty = if derived && rank == 1 && tried > 1 { format!(" — empty for each of {tried} derived rows: give it yours in {}", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
                    println!("      {label:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}  {state} in {ms:.1} ms{shown}{empty}", m.examined, m.index_entries, m.rule_probes, m.steps, m.heap_bytes, m.output_bytes);
                }
                // A refusal the persona's own case expects is what the builder
                // asked for: say so instead of suggesting a case that exists.
                Err(refusal) if cases.iter().any(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())) => {
                    expected += 1;
                    let at = cases.iter().find(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())).map(|case| case.at.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
                    println!("      {label:<28} refused {} as expected ({at}){}", refusal.code, if source == Some(at) { arguments } else { shown });
                }
                Err(refusal) if refusal.family == "rule" => {
                    // A program's own `require` refusing this persona is
                    // information, not a failure: the persona is not in that
                    // group. The caller chose nothing on this pass, whether
                    // the arguments were derived or there are none (wave 5
                    // lane 059: an argument-free gate counted as denied).
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — the derived pass never fails on the program's own require.
                    refused_derived += 1;
                    if let Some(at) = source {
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on your arguments{shown} — the case at {at} runs below", refusal.code);
                    } else if derived {
                        let rows = if tried > 1 { format!(" (each of {tried} rows)") } else { String::new() };
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on derived arguments{shown}{rows} — give it yours in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"args\":{{..}}}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE);
                    } else {
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} by its own require (not a failure: {persona} is not admitted) — pin it in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"expect\":\"{}\"}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE, refusal.code);
                    }
                }
                Err(refusal) => {
                    failures += 1;
                    println!("      {label:<28} denied {}: {}{shown}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for line in near.iter().take(10) { println!("near ceiling: {line}"); }
    if near.len() > 10 { println!("near ceiling: {} more query/persona reads passed half a ceiling without a cut", near.len() - 10); }
    for line in &skipped {
        println!("      not run: {line} — add a row of that table to the fixture, or a case in {}", test_cases::FILE);
    }
    // One line a script or an agent can read: derived refusals and unrun
    // queries are not failures, but they are not coverage either.
    let generated_only=!populated_fixture&&!options.flags.contains_key("source");
    if generated_only {println!("test not covered: no authored business cases; generated-fixture only");}
    let no_cases = passed + failed_cases == 0 && !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") && !generated_only;
    let security_failed = security_campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()) || !security_campaign.boundary_failures.is_empty() || !security_http_ok;
    let verdict = if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { "FAIL" } else if !skipped.is_empty() || held_not_run > 0 { "incomplete" } else { "pass" };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — derived probe refusals are expected exploration, not failed cases.
    println!("test: {complete} complete, {capped} capped, {expected} refused as your cases expect, {refused_derived} probe refusals on derived arguments (expected), {} not run, {failures} denied{} — {verdict}",
        skipped.len() + held_not_run, if passed + failed_cases > 0 { format!("; cases: {passed} passed, {failed_cases} failed") } else if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") { format!("; no {} (see snapback4 guide testing)", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() });
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — query-sweep reads are not application business cases.
    if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
        println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":{},\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}", passed + failed_cases);
    }
    if no_cases {
        eprintln!("test: no business cases executed ({skipped_business_cases} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl, or use --fixture-only when fixture loading/query probes are the intended check");
    }
    if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { 1 } else { 0 }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    }
    #[test]
    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let file = root.join("v1.q");
        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], flags: [("schema".into(), file.to_str().unwrap().into())].into(), json: false };
        let selected = sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(selected[0].0, "schema.q");
        let bound = compile(&selected, &options, "schema");
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].program.name, "alive");
        std::fs::write(&file, "not a Quarry backend").unwrap();
        let bound = compile(&sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap(), &options, "schema");
        assert!(!bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| d.site.file == file.to_str().unwrap()));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_baseline_names_original_absolute_location() {
        let root = dir("missing-baseline"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::write(root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "query alive():\n  return true\n".into())]);
        let error = project.write_generated(&bound).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(error.contains(root.join("versions/v1").to_str().unwrap()) && error.contains("does not exist (resolved from") && error.contains("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), "{error}");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn inherited_native_workspace_refuses_before_touching_working_generated_types() {
        let root = dir("workspace"); let stage = dir("workspace-stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion.workspace = true\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "job sweep(): native\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let error = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).err().unwrap();
        assert!(error.contains("workspace-inherited") && error.contains("standalone"), "{error}");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn native_types_are_built_for_selected_source_in_private_tree() {
        let root = dir("native"); let stage = dir("stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir().join("src")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let source = "table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20\n  public 'shared'\njob sweep(): native\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("v2.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let candidate = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        crate::native::build(&candidate.project, &bound).err().expect("private build refuses absent native target"); // no lib target: a private build refusal, never working generated writes
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(candidate.project.native_types()).unwrap().contains("color"));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        let manifest = "[dependencies]\nsdk = { path = \"../sdk\" }\n";
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/sdk")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), manifest).unwrap();
        let relocated = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        let parsed: toml::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(relocated.project.native_manifest()).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed["dependencies"]["sdk"]["path"].as_str().unwrap(), root.join("snapback/sdk").canonicalize().unwrap().to_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_manifest()).unwrap(), manifest);
        drop(relocated);
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        drop(candidate); std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
}

fn rebase_native(text: &str, original: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))?;
    fn inherited(value: &toml::Value) -> bool {
        let marker = |field: &toml::Value| field.get("workspace").and_then(toml::Value::as_bool) == Some(true);
        if value.get("package").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.iter().any(|(key, field)| key != "metadata" && marker(field))) { return true; }
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies"] {
            if value.get(key).and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.values().any(marker)) { return true; }
        }
        value.get("target").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|targets| targets.values().any(inherited))
    }
    if !workspace && inherited(&manifest) { return Err("workspace-inherited native packages require a complete standalone [workspace] under snapback/native; copy its workspace package/dependency declarations before saved-source deployment".into()); }
    fn dependency_group(group: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(dependencies) = group.as_table_mut() {
            for (_, dependency) in dependencies.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(path) = dependency.as_table_mut().and_then(|fields| fields.get_mut("path")) {
                    let relative = path.as_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be a string")?;
                    let absolute = root.join(relative).canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native dependency {relative}: {e}"))?;
                    let absolute = absolute.strip_prefix(source_root).map(|relative| target_root.join(relative)).unwrap_or(absolute.clone());
                    *path = toml::Value::String(absolute.to_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be UTF-8")?.to_owned());
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn groups(table: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies", "replace"] {
            if let Some(group) = table.get_mut(key) { dependency_group(group, root, source_root, target_root)?; }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    groups(&mut manifest, original, source_root, target_root)?;
    for category in ["target", "patch"] {
        if let Some(table) = manifest.get_mut(category).and_then(toml::Value::as_table_mut) {
            for (_, item) in table.iter_mut() {
                if category == "target" { groups(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
                else { dependency_group(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(workspace) = manifest.get_mut("workspace") { groups(workspace, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
    toml::to_string(&manifest).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))
}

fn rebase_tree(target: &Path, original: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(target.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("native manifest: {e}"))?;
    let workspace = manifest.get("workspace").is_some();
    fn walk(directory: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if entry.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() { walk(&entry.path(), source_root, target_root, workspace)?; }
            else if entry.file_name() == "Cargo.toml" {
                let relative = entry.path().strip_prefix(target_root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.to_owned();
                let source = source_root.join(relative).parent().unwrap().to_owned();
                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                let rebased = rebase_native(&text, &source, source_root, target_root, workspace)?;
                std::fs::write(entry.path(), rebased).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    let original = original.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    walk(target, &original, target, workspace)
}
//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{fixture}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{fixture}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {fixture} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }
        (ColumnType::Time, value) => {
            let Some(text) = value.as_str().filter(|text| text.starts_with("now")) else { return Ok(()) };
            let delta = if text == "now" { 0 } else {
                let offset = text.strip_prefix("now").unwrap();
                if !offset.starts_with(['+', '-']) { return Err(format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: expected now or now±duration (ms, s, m, h, d, w)")); }
                crate::dev_clock::offset(offset).map_err(|why| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: {}", why.message))?
            };
            let resolved = now.checked_add(delta).filter(|time| (0..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(time))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?} is outside Unix milliseconds 0..9007199254740991"))?;
            *value = resolved.into();
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn argument_times(kinds: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = args.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = kinds.get(name).and_then(snapback4_core::schema::arg_as_column) {
                time_value(&kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod column_tests {
    use super::*;
    use snapback4_core::{Context, Value};
    #[test]
    fn residency_fixture_import_rebuilds_poisoned_columns() {
        let source=include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q");
        let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
        let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("f1a-fixture-{}",std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        {
            let files=[("posts",r#"{"id":"p","at":1,"likeCount":999,"selected":999,"total":999}"#),
                ("likes",r#"{"id":"l","postId":"p","active":true,"amount":7}"#)];
            for index in [0,1] {std::fs::write(root.join(format!("{}.jsonl",files[index].0)),files[index].1).unwrap();}
            let options=Options {positionals:vec![],json:true,flags:[("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into()),("residency".into(),String::new())].into()};
            let project=Project {root:root.clone()};
            let mut engine=engine(&project,&bound,&options).unwrap();
            let row=engine.query("postRow",Context::new("alice",[("id".into(),Value::Id("p".into()))].into()).at(1000)).unwrap().data;
            assert_eq!(["likeCount","selected","total"].map(|field|row.field(field).unwrap().as_int().unwrap()),[1,1,7]);
            assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("posts").unwrap(),vec![row]);
            for m in &engine.schema().maintains {
                let rows=engine.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(rows.len(),1);
                assert_eq!(rows[0].field("value").unwrap(),&Value::Int(if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column=="total" {7} else {1}));
            }
            for (table,_) in files {std::fs::remove_file(root.join(format!("{table}.jsonl"))).unwrap();}
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir(&root).unwrap();
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod order_tests {
    use super::*;
    // `items` sorts before `lists`: the child's file is read first, yet the
    // directory loads as one batch with parents ordered before children.
    #[test]
    fn read_through_child_file_before_parent_file_loads() {
        let source = "use personas alice, bob\n\ntable lists:\n  owner: principal\n  title: text 1..80\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by owner\n  sync to .owner\n\ntable items:\n  parent: lists\n  text: text 1..200\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by parent\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("fixture-order-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("items.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000C01\",\"parent\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"text\":\"wash\"}\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("lists.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"owner\":\"dev:alice\",\"title\":\"Home\"}\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(), root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let engine = engine(&project, &bound, &options);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let engine = engine.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("items").unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("lists").unwrap().len(), 1);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod relative_tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;
    #[test]
    fn relative_time_is_typed_checked_and_does_not_rewrite_text_or_json() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time\n  optionalAt: time ?\n  title: text <=80\n  payload: json <=200\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut table = bound.schema.tables["notes"].clone();
        use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
        table.columns.insert("times".into(), ColumnType::List { of: Box::new(ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(ColumnType::Time))), max: 4 });
        let now = 1_000_000_000;
        for (text, expected) in [("now", now), ("now-1h", now-3_600_000), ("now+2d", now+172_800_000), ("now+1ms", now+1)] {
            let mut row = json!({"at":text, "optionalAt":text, "title":"now-1h", "payload":{"time":"now"}});
            relative_times(&table, &mut row, now).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(row, json!({"at":expected,"optionalAt":expected,"title":"now-1h","payload":{"time":"now"}}));
        }
        for text in ["nowsoon", "now+0h", "now+1.5h", "now-9999999999999999999h", "now-100w"] {
            let error = relative_times(&table, &mut json!({"at":text}), now).unwrap_err();
            assert!(error.contains("E_FIXTURE_TIME") && error.contains(text), "{error}");
        }
        let mut null = json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null});
        relative_times(&table, &mut null, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(null, json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null}));
        let mut list = json!({"times":["now", null, "now-1s"]});
        relative_times(&table, &mut list, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(list, json!({"times":[now, null, now-1000]}));
    }
    #[test]
    fn seed_defaults_and_relative_rows_share_the_captured_clock() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time default now\n  ends: time\n  expire at .ends\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nquery one():\n  return notes[\"n\"]\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("rel3-relative-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("notes.jsonl"), r#"{"id":"n","ends":"now+1ms"}"#).unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let mut engine = engine_at(&Project { root:root.clone() }, &bound, &options, now).unwrap();
        let row = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(row.field("at").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now));
        assert_eq!(row.field("ends").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now+1));
        let gone = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now+1)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(gone, snapback4_core::Value::Null);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
}
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
            |pending| { if let Some(candidate)=candidate.as_deref_mut() {candidate.pending=pending;} },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}
