//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{fixture}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{fixture}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {fixture} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#132-generated-initial-worlds-and-password-only-test — generated initialization is explicit.
use super::*;
/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
fn proposals(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut people=Vec::new();
    for (table,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        let role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).into_iter().filter(|c|c=="role"||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table).is_some()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        for column in role_columns{
            {
                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
            }
        }
    }
    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")&&p!="service:security") {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
    }}
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){grants(&t.rules.read,&mut grant_tables);}
    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||t.rules.read.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
        let mut population=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..counts[table]{
            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
            let mut principal=0;
            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0{Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
                match kind.required(){
                    C::Principal=>{
                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
                    },
                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
                    _=>{},
                }
                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
            }
            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
        }
        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
    }
    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,mut row) in pending {
            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let attempt=(||{
                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
            })();
            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.columns.contains_key("role")||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name).is_some()&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
        for (table,row) in pending{
            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
            let gate=&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert;
            if !actors.iter().any(|actor|matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable))) {retry.push((table,row));continue;}
            let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
            if candidate.seed_at([(table.clone(),seeded)],NOW).is_ok(){engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
        }
        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    }
    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
    let mut attempts=0usize;
    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
        let before=readback(&engine)?;
        for bp in &creators{
            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
            let current=readback(&engine)?;
            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(p,&current,&actors,out);
            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &actors{
                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
                attempts+=1;
                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
                }
            }}
        }
        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
    }
    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
}
fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
    match row{Value::Row(fields)=>Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect()),_=>row.clone()}
}
//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    (tier(code),match code{
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"=>0,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"=>1,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"=>3,"N_BROAD_READ"=>4,"N_SECURITY_INTENT"=>5,_=>2,
    })
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",

exec
/bin/bash -lc "timeout 10s sed -n '1350,1395p' llp/3000.036.004-security-invariants-and-exposure.rfc.md; timeout 10s sed -n '1,205p' snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/histories.rs" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4
 succeeded in 0ms:
admitted after substituting that role, descending, then deterministic role
order; the source location, effective gate and recipe remain in the note.
The existing 200-schema false-refusal bar and explicit origin labels remain.

Only failure-eligible origins are persisted or reloaded, with their exact origin
and witness. Supplemental synthetic observations are never promoted by caching.
A later witness-free or interrupted run on identical inputs does not erase
established failures: records merge prior eligible witnesses, deduplicating by
site/observer/viewer/effect/origin; a source, fixture or executable change is
the invalidation boundary. Full phase completion and known violations are
separate metadata. Saved-source deploys cannot borrow the working-source record;
the demand must name the corresponding `test --source <candidate> --fixture ...`
selection, and use matching selected-source evidence, never the working-source record.

Distinct committed operation/viewer paths are not collapsed merely because
their lengths match. A one-step foundUnit witness cannot hide registerStaff,
and a one-step provision cannot hide linkUnit. Deduplication preserves each
operation/viewer path; repeated values on that path remain finite sampling.
A declined duplicate never mutates a previous witness's operation or arguments.
Relationship candidates prioritize actual admitted principal transfers from the
initial world, with explicit bounded probing, followed by ordinary replay and
observation. No preflight-only candidate is itself a refutation.

Retained-grant lifetime advice no longer requires declared role intent when a
grant writer depends on a mutable role roster but the read branch does not
consult that roster. Its wording is conditional: grants can survive role
changes; confirm whether current-role intent is required. This does not infer
a privacy specification or refute an invariant. It restores 019's pointer while
keeping lifetime advice out of the escalation/failing-witness tier.
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}

    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}

//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.

pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,options:&Options)->Report{
    let mut report=security::analyze(bound);
    if let Ok(sources)=project.sources(){security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,bound);}
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)};
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty(){
        let mut selection=options.clone();
        if !selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),project.root.join("fixtures").to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)},
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    if let Some(record)=record::matching(project,options) {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(match project.compile(){Ok(b)=>b,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}},options.json);
    let mut full=options.clone();full.flags.insert("security-full".into(),String::new());
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&full);
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::to_string(&report).unwrap());}
    else {print!("{}",report.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    exposure(report);
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&!v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted, {} unproved; {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted);
    for v in report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted){
        if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample&&c.reachability.is_reachable(){eprintln!("  {} FAIL {} via {} ({:?}; initial fixture assumptions apply)",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.reachability);}
    }
    if verbose {eprint!("{}",report.text_with_all(true));}
    if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  run snapback4 test for full campaigns; {} coverage gaps (--verbose/JSON for details)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  run snapback4 audit for column audiences, grant writers and {} uncovered exposures",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options){
    let changed=prior.is_some_and(|old|bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(name,t)|old.schema.tables.get(name).is_none_or(|o|serde_json::to_value(&o.rules).ok()!=serde_json::to_value(&t.rules).ok()))||old.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|!bound.schema.tables.contains_key(t)));
    if !changed{return;}
    let report=security::analyze(bound);exposure(&report);
    let selection=options.flags.get("schema").map(|s|format!(" --source {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let fixture=options.flags.get("fixture").map(|s|format!(" --fixture {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    match record::matching(project,options){
        Some(record) if record.complete=>eprintln!("security rules changed: matching test campaign recorded; {} reachable refutations; {} coverage gaps; {} independent invariants (finite, not proof)",record.violations.len(),record.coverage_gaps,bound.invariants.len()),
        _=>eprintln!("security rules changed: run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment; no matching completed campaign for this source, fixtures and CLI; {} independent invariants",bound.invariants.len()),
    }
}

    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}

}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{

    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
    }
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
}
fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }

fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
        (name.clone(),clean)
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
        }
    }}}
    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    Ok(engine)
}
fn validate_fixture(bound:&Bound,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<(),String>{
    let actual=readback(engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(())
}

fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{of:Some(inner),..}=>Value::Array(vec![one(inner,v)?]),A::ListRange{of:Some(inner),min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|one(inner,v)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),A::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),_=>return None,
    })}
    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

fn materialize_arguments(engine:&mut Engine,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,values:&BTreeMap<String,Value>,viewer:&str)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,String>{
    fn one(e:&mut Engine,k:&ir::ArgType,v:&Value,viewer:&str)->Result<Value,String>{Ok(match (k,v){
        (ir::ArgType::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>one(e,inner,v,viewer)?,
        (ir::ArgType::List{of:Some(inner),..}|ir::ArgType::ListRange{of:Some(inner),..},Value::Array(xs))=>Value::Array(xs.iter().map(|v|one(e,inner,v,viewer)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        (ir::ArgType::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>e.store_asset(viewer,&PNG,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.column_value(),
        (ir::ArgType::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(e,viewer,types)?,
        _=>v.clone(),
    })}
    values.iter().map(|(n,v)|one(engine,&args[n],v,viewer).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    let eligible=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)&&!p.program.native).count();
    let quota=(8192/eligible.max(1)).clamp(1,512);let mut remaining=8192usize;
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
        let mut exercised=0;
        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for viewer in &viewers{
                if exercised==quota||remaining==0{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
                exercised+=1;remaining-=1;
                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
                }else{
                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
