fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return Vec::new();};
    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
            let mut targets=Vec::new();
            for case in cases{
                let mut args=case.clone();
                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
                }}
                if columns.iter().any(|c|args.get(c).is_some_and(|v|row.field(c).ok()==Some(v))){continue;}
                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
            }
            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
            for actor in actors{
                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
                if !campaign.reserve(1){return out;}
                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
            }
            groups.push(row_cases);
        }
    }
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
    out
}
// Prioritize actually admitted relationship changes rather than filling the
// history cap with cross-products rejected by a recipient-role require.
fn relationship_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if !p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))||!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables[t].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))){return Vec::new();}
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut attempts=0;
    let Ok(mut judge)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return candidates;};
    for actor in actors{
        let allowed=writes.iter().any(|(t,kind)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|{
            let table=&bound.schema.tables[t];let gates=if *kind=="insert"{vec![&table.rules.insert]}else{vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update]};
            gates.into_iter().any(|gate|matches!(judge.fixture_policy(gate,actor,Some(row),row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable)))
        }));
        if !allowed{continue;}
        for args in cases{
            if attempts>=128||candidates.len()>=16||!out.reserve(1){return candidates;}attempts+=1;
            let Ok(mut e)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
            let Ok(materialized)=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,args,actor)else{continue;};
            if !matches!(e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,materialized).at(NOW)),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){continue;}
            let Ok(after)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(t).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|bound.schema.tables[t].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))){
                candidates.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));
            }
        }
    }candidates
}
pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
        }}
        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
        }}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
    for group in &groups {
        let p=group[0].0;
        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
    }
    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
        }}
    }
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
                }
            },_=>{}
        }}}
        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
    }
    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
            let mut k=0;
            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
        }
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent&&n<singles{
                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
                        });
                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))

    }

    if let Some(error) = crate::exact_device::inspect(&project.root, &bound.schema).into_iter().find_map(Result::err) {

        return 1;
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    let security = security::report(&project, &bound, options);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.3 — check judges adoption against the dev store's active generation and rows, read-only
    match adoption::diagnostics(&project, &bound.schema, options.flags.get("store"), options.json || options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
        Ok((problems, notes)) => { bound.diagnostics.extend(problems); bound.notes.extend(notes); },
        Err(error) => { failed_check(&bound, options.json, "E_STORE", format!("cannot check store adoption: {error}")); return 2; }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    for diagnostic in readiness::notes(&project, &bound, options.flags.get("fixture")) {
        if diagnostic.code == "E_FIXTURE_TIME" { bound.diagnostics.push(diagnostic); }
        else { bound.notes.push(diagnostic); }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        return 1;
    }
    bound.notes.extend(snapback4_lang::bind::residency::change_notes(&bound, &residency::previous(&project)));
    check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") || !options.flags.contains_key("residency") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&!options.flags.contains_key("residency") => {
            snapback4_core::Store::memory().and_then(|store|snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),fixture_now)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
        },
        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    };
    let mut engine = match opened {
        Ok(engine) => engine,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    };
    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
        if !verbose {

exec

//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn reserve(out:&mut Campaign,cost:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(cost){return false;}
    if out.column_observer_calls+cost>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 call/world reservations; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return false;}
    out.column_observer_calls+=cost;true
}
fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
}
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let old=match row.field(column){
        Ok(value)=>value.clone(),
        Err(_) if kind.is_optional()&&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column).is_none_or(|d|matches!(d,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Null)))=>Value::Null,
        Err(_)=>return None,
    };
    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,&old)?};
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn same_base_world(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,primary:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,alternate:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,key:&Value)->bool{
    bound.schema.tables.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).all(|(table,definition)|{
        let empty=Vec::new();let rows=primary.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);let other=alternate.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);
        rows.len()==other.len()&&rows.iter().all(|old|other.iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
            let (Value::Row(a),Value::Row(b))=(old,new)else{return false;};
            a.keys().chain(b.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==Some(key))).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
        }))
    })
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other
        },
        None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed value and, for media, a coupled verified fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;},
    };
    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    let ledger;
    let fixture=if kind.contains_asset(){
        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};
        let mut prepared=match fork(bound,base,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
        }
        ledger=prepared;Some(&ledger)
    }else{fixture};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let observing=histories::observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&histories::trace_key(&v.history)==histories::trace_key(trace.map(|(_,s)|s).unwrap_or(&[]))){continue;}

        {
            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let p=&bp.program;
            let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
            if !observing.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(p,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if !reserve(out,12){return;}
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,BTreeMap<String,Value>),String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly))||matches!(t.columns[column].required(),C::Image{once:true,..});
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
                };
                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok((aa,pa,aa_args)),Ok((bb,pb,bb_args))) if aa==a&&bb==b&&pa==primary_rows&&pb==alternate_rows&&aa_args==primary_args&&bb_args==alternate_args){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: repeated column inputs/worlds/output are unstable or unavailable",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                let actual_row=primary_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                let actual_alternate=alternate_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:primary_args,viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:actual_row.clone(),old:Some(actual_alternate.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||primary_rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:alternate_rows})});
                continue 'actors;
            }
        }
    }
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
        for variant in 0..16{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
    }
}

pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
    }
}

//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership is required for every supplied owned field;
// only an exclusively owned patch bypasses an unnamed row update gate.
fn write_admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->RuleExpr{
    let touched=fields.iter().filter(|f|!f.spread&&f.name!="id").map(|f|f.name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let owned_only=!touched.is_empty()&&!fields.iter().any(|f|f.spread)&&touched.iter().all(|f|table.written_by.contains_key(*f));
    let gate=match op{
        ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.clone(),
        ast::WriteOp::Update if owned_only&&!table.update_transition=>RuleExpr::Allow,
        ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),
        _=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    };
    let mut owners=table.written_by.iter().filter(|(field,authority)|touched.contains(field.as_str())||op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&!authority.audience&&table.rules.update.needs_field_value(field,&table.columns[*field])).map(|(_,authority)|RuleExpr::Eq(if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{RuleTerm::Field(authority.owner.clone())}else{RuleTerm::OldField(authority.owner.clone())},RuleTerm::Viewer)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    owners.push(RuleExpr::Or(gate.disjuncts().iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).map(|r|(*r).clone()).collect()));
    RuleExpr::And(owners)
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert,fields)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update,fields);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&global_principal(bound,roster).is_some_and(|column|kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column)))==Ok(true)){return false;}
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic,fields).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic,fields));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let roles=role_values(bound,roster,column);
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.into()),RuleTerm::Field(column.into())))==Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    let mut ranked=roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let score=|role:&&String|bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]).filter(|rule|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(role.as_str()),None))==Ok(true)).count();
    ranked.sort_by_key(|role|(std::cmp::Reverse(score(role)),role.as_str()));
    for target in ranked{
        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
            if !admits{continue;}
            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)]{
                let granted=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,caller,None);
                if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&granted)!=Ok(true){continue;}
                if !dynamic&&!(matches!(effect,"read"|"delete")||!role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()){continue;}
                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            }}
        }
    }None
}

pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
                        });
                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
                    }
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

## 3. Sound proof boundary

For a table effect, prove `admission => policy` for **every database snapshot**,
row image, ordinary authenticated viewer (including service seats) and clock.
The engine system principal is explicitly outside this quantification: it is
the trusted seed/import/job boundary, never an ordinary user or an implicit
service-seat exemption. Tables touched by system/native/job/worker paths remain
unproved absent a reviewed reduction, and audit lists those writers. This overapproximates every reachable history:
an old assignment and a changed current role are independent facts. Grant
creation never proves a current role. Required role predicates must be true at
the access boundary. No fixture-specific assumption enters a proof.

Translate Boolean connectives compositionally, retaining true/false/unknown
truth for atomic predicates. Unknown never admits and `not unknown` is unknown.
Atomic facts are keyed by their full bound structure, including table, index,
keys, named equalities and old/next image. Symmetric equality may canonicalize by operand reordering only, preserving
Value variants (Id is not Text), table, index, keys, image and predicate kind;
other equalities and probes stay independent. A finite exhaustive implication
check over this abstraction is sound because it includes every concrete
valuation, even impossible ones. Cap atoms/work; cap exhaustion is unproved.
Do not add database consistency axioms without a separate soundness argument.
Never drop, assume true/false, or simplify an unsupported admission atom;
keep it opaque with its full identity or report unproved. Concrete leaf
semantics are the current `evaluate_leaf`/`term`, not stale Lean open points:
null equals null, null differs from a non-null literal, Id differs from Text,
noninteger ordered comparison returns false, and a missing live reference
marks unknown (including under negation). Missing ordinary fields currently
load null; missing reference and missing image are not conflated. Any semantic
mismatch with the independent model is named, never normalized away. Runtime
errors do not establish an admitted result; probe/storage/schema failure
cannot be used as a successful counterexample or a proof fact.

The Lean addition proves a three-valued Kleene kernel with admission only
on Readable and the exact theorem: exhaustive checking implies that for every
valuation, eval(admission) = Readable implies eval(policy) = Readable; the Quarry-to-kernel translation and runtime boundary remain reviewed
Rust obligations, not a claim of whole-platform formal verification.

For read intent, admission includes the ordinary read rule and every bypass:
- point/list/view/filter/count/exists and live refetch use ruled reads;
## 4. Counterexamples and generated tests

Generate deterministic challenges from schema types, policy atoms and fixture
rows. Use in-memory stores; never leave SQLite sidecars under target. Vary the
viewer across owner/other-user/other-role/revoked-role/unauthenticated classes,
policy literals, principal arguments and absent optional values. Existing role
rows and retained grant rows form revoked-role snapshots. Do not call an
arbitrary consistent snapshot a *reachable* application-operation sequence.

Concrete counterexamples must replay the engine's actual rule admission and
show the policy false, recording viewer, effect, row, supporting rows and rule
path. The absence of a concrete witness when implication fails is **unproved**.
Generated tests are a separate executable check, run by default `test`, with
coverage counts and named unsupported cases. They use isolated state so they
cannot corrupt the fixture business tests. For operations, derive arguments
from fixtures and schema, substitute other principals and roles, and compare
admitted effects/observations against the declared intent. Merely succeeding
as another user is not a failure when policy permits that operation. Signed-out
network admission cannot be tested by inventing a signed-in principal: label
that boundary separately. No finite campaign promises arbitrary sequence
coverage. `test` fails on replayed violations; unproved coverage is explicit.

## 5. Exposure report

`audit` and `check --audit`, both with `--json`, produce a stable structured
report. This is a conservative permission map, not a claim every listed path
is reachable. Enumerate each table/effect, row predicate, audience-readable
and owner-private columns, operations touching it, sync/asset/derived paths,
and inherited dependencies. List probes and the insert/update writers of the
probed grant columns; include transitive dependencies with a visited set.
Enumerate role alternatives found in rule probes/literals, including generic
signed-in principals and service seats. Unmodeled role predicates retain their
full expression rather than collapsing to an invented role name. Normal
`check` lists broad signed-in reads and tables with no declared intent, with
`guide security` as the next step. Do not claim `allow` admits signed-out
requests; authentication precedes the operation.

## 6. Bakeoff repairs

Add advisory lints (not unsound policy proofs) for grants lacking current-role
rechecks, argument writes into reader-grant columns, open `created` bootstrap
of globally trusted authority (extend N_VACUOUS_RULE), optional evidence, and
sweep-only status gates. Name the rule/column/operation and the safe recipe.
An existence-oracle advisory compares distinct authored absent/denied codes;
it must acknowledge the read rule may already hide the row. Do not silently
rewrite authored codes, which are a public application contract; privacy intent
must account for the resulting observation or remain unproved.

Text minimums count non-whitespace Unicode scalar values (`char::is_whitespace`);
maximums continue to count all scalar values. This applies to arguments, rows,
optional/list-contained text on server/device, without changing stored bytes.
Document the behavioral change and test ASCII/Unicode whitespace and mixed
text. Media stays optional only when the product allows omission; required
photos use required `image` or `[image] 1..N`, with existing content validation.

Add adversarial compiler/engine tests for proof soundness, unknown predicates,
atom limits, indirect blockers, role revocation, grants and named transitions.
Extend the independent Lean model for the propositional kernel. Include
regressions for owned-field bypass with deny update, cascade and set-null
against denied child rules, raw-rule existence observations, and disconnected
server/replica role revocation. Pin original
and repaired wave-16 reproductions with their intended policy; retain exact
source provenance, and explain ambiguous specs (045) and missing transitions
(015), never manufacture evidence for an operation the source lacks.
Run all 50 wave-16 and 100 wave-15 schemas, reporting pre-existing diagnostics,
new warnings and unproved assertions separately. Unannotated applications have
no new false proof/refusal claims; they receive discoverability notes. Include
actual default generated-test execution, not only parsing or lint counts.

Guide cards, README and starters lead to `guide security`; runnable new Quarry
examples must also compile using a freshly packed CLI. Add 0.4.32 guide changes.
Run affected tests, full requested crate/client/differential qualifications,
wasm measurement and budget (measurement + 500 B with reason), ref/docs/root
checks. Record exact commands, results and any genuine environment limits in
WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md. Do not count an incomplete qualification as green.

## 8. Operation and general row assertions (SEC refinement)
### 12.1 Column read intent

`invariant table.column read only P` protects the value, computations over it,
value-dependent counts/existence/refusals, copied values, assets and delivery.
It does not forbid observing an otherwise public row's existence independently
of the protected value. Binding validates the column and preserves its identity
in every verdict and audit. Effective payload authority is row read AND any
owner-private field gate. Pure projections excluding the field may eliminate
that payload path; predicates, group/order keys, derived values and copied
fields remain observations. Unknown flow stays unproved. A synthetic whole-row
read cannot refute a projection-only column policy: column counterexamples
execute an authored observer in two schema-valid worlds differing only in that
column, with policy false in both. Outputs must differ reproducibly. Maintained
values are recomputed; column-sensitive counts/existence are included. Separate
obligations name unresolved raw-probe, wire, device and opaque flows. A direct
column theorem never silently clears those obligations. Per-column audit
reports effective audience plus operation/derived observers, not only its type.

### 12.2 Reproducible campaigns and reachability

Generated seed ingress contains only authored base rows/fields. Never re-seed
maintained targets or engine-recorded fields. Generated lifetime samples are
live at the campaign clock; principal fields vary independently. Rejected
snapshots are recorded as unsupported, not successful attempts. Existing
fixture histories instead fork the complete SQLite store and asset ledger into
owned isolated memory stores; captures, deadlines, maintained values, lifetimes
and actual media identities survive unchanged. No source store is modified.
Temporary asset directories belong to Store and disappear on drop.

Actors include all principal values in fixtures. Required/optional references
range over fixture rows, not only the first. Short histories visit first steps
breadth first before extending them under a reported work cap. Read and row
assertions are checked after each step. Write/operation assertions judge only
actual committed changes, correct insert/update/delete arms and pre-write
policy state; ambiguous multiple writes, implicit effects or unstable policy
need executed-effect tracing and remain unsupported. A finite trace is never
an induction proof for always or arbitrary histories.

Every counterexample has a machine-readable origin: authored trace from trusted
fixture, trusted-seed-only, or synthetic operation. Only the first establishes
application reachability from the recorded trusted starting state and fails
normal check/test. Seed/synthetic witnesses remain refutations of all-snapshot
claims, visible with --all and JSON, without failing acceptance. Unproved
reachability is never asserted unreachable as a mathematical theorem. Check
loads the same fixture base for reachable histories as test, without HTTP or
business-case execution. A stronger witness must replace a synthetic witness
for the same declaration. --all exposes all witnesses and never changes which
ones fail. No unreachable refutation may print pass/ok beside exit 1.

### 12.3 Actionable authority advice

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies authored caller operations that write role
or group grants trusted by later rules while the effective admission does not
establish independent authority to confer that particular grant. Include
self-signup roles and transitions where a caller can confer a role they lack.
A trusted service seat or an independently authorized administrator may appoint
other roles; holding an unrelated ordinary role does not establish delegation.
Advice names the operation, grant and gate and points to guide staging's
operator first-admin recipe. It is advice, never an implicit runtime refusal
or a proof of malicious intent. Legitimate open enrollment can be intentional.

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE requires an actually mutable/revocable current-role
population and a retained principal/grant branch lacking that role recheck;
cover read AND write branches. Immutable/static role populations do not fire.
N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE excludes public populations and caller-owned role lookups;
only an absent/hidden distinction crossing a narrower private audience warrants
advice. Every security note has a real source location and contributes to the
reported note count. Rule printing preserves connective precedence. Identity
429 responses include a conservative retry delay covering the rate window;
the existing limits and authentication semantics are unchanged.

### 12.4 Evidence

Qualification uses read-only copies of all ten wave-17 high lanes and their
spec-authored invariant sets, plus all 150 earlier schemas. Report caught,
partial and not caught separately; all-snapshot witnesses alone do not count
as a reachable detection. Preserve bad/fixed controls, note counts and source
hashes. The open qualification-gap ticket remains open for unresolved whole
proof/device/opaque obligations. No zero-hole claim follows from finite tests.

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Options {
    pub positionals: Vec<String>,
    pub flags: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub json: bool,
}

/// Explicit control ports remain an intentional selection; otherwise discover
/// a unique, identity-validated project host (persisted or temporary).
pub(crate) fn dev_address(options: &Options) -> Result<(std::net::SocketAddr, Option<String>), String> {
    control_address(options, false)
}

pub(crate) fn registered_dev_address(options: &Options) -> Result<(std::net::SocketAddr, Option<String>), String> {
    control_address(options, true)
}

fn control_address(options: &Options, registered_only: bool) -> Result<(std::net::SocketAddr, Option<String>), String> {
    if let Some(text)=options.flags.get("port") {
        let port=text.parse::<u16>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or("E_INPUT: --port must be an integer in 1..65535")?;
        return Ok((std::net::SocketAddr::from((std::net::Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST,port)),None));
    }
    let cwd=std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let project=Project::locate(&cwd).ok_or("E_INPUT: cannot discover dev outside a project; pass --port N using the port dev printed")?;
    let host=if registered_only { adoption::registered_host(&project,options) } else { adoption::host(&project,options) }?
        .ok_or("E_INPUT: no running dev discovered for this project; start dev or pass --port N using the port dev printed")?;
    let address=host["address"].as_str().ok_or("dev did not report an address")?.parse().map_err(|e|format!("invalid dev address: {e}"))?;
    Ok((address,host["instance"].as_str().map(str::to_owned)))
}

pub fn parse(args: &[String]) -> Options {
    let mut positionals = Vec::new();
    let mut flags = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut json = false;
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "-h" { flags.insert("help".into(), String::new()); continue; }
        if arg == "--" { positionals.extend(iter.cloned()); break; }
        if arg == "--json" || arg == "--json-objects" {
            json = true;
            if arg == "--json-objects" { flags.insert("json-objects".into(), String::new()); }
        } else if let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") {
            // A switch takes no value; any other flag takes the next token
            // unless that token is itself a flag.
            let (name, value) = match flag.split_once('=') {
                Some((name, value)) => (name.to_owned(), value.to_owned()),
                None if crate::cli_flags::switch(flag) || ( !matches!(flag, "find" | "section") && iter.peek().is_none_or(|next| next.starts_with("--")) ) => (flag.to_owned(), String::new()),
                None => (flag.to_owned(), iter.next().cloned().unwrap_or_default()),
            };
            if name == "as" && matches!(args.first().map(String::as_str), Some("test" | "check")) {
                flags.entry(name).and_modify(|prior: &mut String| { prior.push(','); prior.push_str(&value); }).or_insert(value);
            } else { flags.insert(name, value); }
        } else {
            positionals.push(arg.clone());
        }
    }
    Options { positionals, flags, json }
}

fn project_or_exit() -> Project {
    match std::env::current_dir().ok().and_then(|dir| Project::locate(&dir)) {
        Some(project) => project,
        None => {
            eprintln!("no snapback/schema.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` to start one");
            std::process::exit(2);
        }
    }
}

fn compile_or_exit(project: &Project, json: bool) -> Bound {
    let bound = match project.compile() {
        Ok(bound) => bound,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("cannot read snapback/: {error}");
            std::process::exit(2);
        }
    };
    compiled_or_exit(bound, json)
}

fn compiled_or_exit(bound: Bound, json: bool) -> Bound {
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        // These one-shot verbs retain their complete diagnostic output.
        report_diagnostics(&bound, json, false, true);
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
    bound
}

fn report_diagnostics(bound: &Bound, json: bool, explain_repeats: bool, verbose: bool) {
    if json {
        println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": bound.diagnostics, "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": [] }));
        return;
    }
    report_problems(bound, "check", "", explain_repeats, verbose);
}

fn failed_check(bound: &Bound, json: bool, code: &str, message: String) {
    if json { println!("{}", json!({"ok": false, "problems": [{"code": code, "message": message}], "notes": bound.notes, "newNotes": bound.notes, "acknowledgedNotes": []})); }
    else { eprintln!("{message}"); }
}

pub(crate) fn report_problems(bound: &Bound, label: &str, suffix: &str, explain_repeats: bool, verbose: bool) {
    let (reports, hidden) = problem_reports(bound, explain_repeats, verbose);
    let shown = reports.len();
    eprintln!("{label}: {shown} problem{}{suffix}", if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
    for report in reports { eprintln!("{}", crate::presentation::indexes(&bound.schema, &report, verbose)); }
    if hidden > 0 { eprintln!("{label}: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"); }
}

//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
        }
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}

//! Effective column payload audience; unresolved observer edges stay explicit.
use super::*;
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — a private owner gate narrows only the column payload.
pub(super) fn admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,column:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let mut rules=vec![super::temporal::admission(table)];
    if let Some(owner)=column.and_then(|c|table.written_by.get(c)).filter(|o|!o.audience){
        rules.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));

    RuleExpr::And(rules)
}

//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
}
pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    match op {
        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    }
}
pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
        } else {edge};
        facts.push(edge);
    }
    RuleExpr::And(facts)
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
        _=>None,
    }}
    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
        }
        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    }
    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Insert,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
}

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/why/N_DELETE_RESTRICTED.md:3:N_DELETE_RESTRICTED means a mutation or job deletes a row that another table references through a reference with no declared action — directly, or through a table its cascades reach. Since 0.5 an undeclared reference is `on delete restrict`: the delete refuses E_REFERENCED while a live row still references the target. Before 0.5 the referencing rows stayed and named a deleted row. Declare what you mean on the referencing column and the note goes away: `on delete cascade` (with `cap N by <column>`) deletes them with the target, `on delete set null` clears an optional reference no rule reads, `on delete keep` keeps them as before, and `on delete restrict` keeps the refusal. Anyone whose insert rule admits a referencing row can hold the target under restrict, so a reference other principals create to your rows is usually `cascade`. A guard that proves no referencing row exists also silences it: `require bookingsBySlot[slot] = 0` on the deleted key, at the top level before the delete, over an unfiltered `count bookings by .slot`, a `sum` view over a count-like column (every insert rule branch requires `.beds >= 1`, the column is immutable, no job writes the table; over any other column a zero sum proves nothing and the note says to count the rows), or a status the deleted row must hold that every child insert flips away from (`require a.status = 'available'` where each insert follows `update artworks[artwork] { status: 'sold' }`). snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/why/E_REFERENCE_ACTION.md:3:E_REFERENCE_ACTION means an `on delete` declaration the column cannot carry. `on delete` belongs on a reference column (its type names a table) between two ordinary durable tables: ephemeral tables, engine-maintained views and receipt or timeline bookkeeping keep their own lifetimes. `cascade` and `set null` write another table's rows, so their target may not be append only, hold field-owned (`written by`) columns, belong to a timeline, or have writes without a fixed price; a cascade may not reach its own table again (a cycle, including a self-reference). `set null` needs an optional reference (`?`) that carries no authority anywhere: no rule of any table reads it, no sync audience or horizon uses it, and it is not immutable, field-owned, a sequence parent or the root of a recorded or maintained path — clearing such a column would change who may read or write a row, which the deleter was never authorized to do. `keep` is refused on `read through` (a read-through child lives within its parent), and `cascade` on `read through` needs a child that never expires. Choose `cascade`, `restrict` or `keep` instead, as the message's rewrite shows. guide writes.

/// The parent's read rule as probes of the parent row through `reference`:
//! Rule evaluation and the static facts about it that the engine hoists: a
//! rule that is proven true by the scanned prefix (`decided_true`) costs no
//! probes; a rule whose deciding fields are pinned by the prefix (`uniform`) is
//! decided once for the whole range. Copied from Snapback 2's uniformity
//! analysis (LLP 2000.000 §5.1); the certificate that priced these is gone,
//! the analysis is not.

use crate::ir::{Bound, Expr, IndexKeyExpr, Input, Merge, Scan};
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Table};
use crate::value::Value;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Visibility {
    /// One verdict serves every row of the scanned prefix.
    pub uniform: bool,
    /// That verdict is `true` before any row is read.
    pub decided_true: bool,
}

pub fn scan_visibility(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Visibility {
    let Some(table) = schema.table(&scan.table) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&scan.prefix).find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten());
    visibility(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned)

}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — a missing upsert may insert only when the complete held group and its key decide that no hidden target can exist.
pub fn upsert_absence(schema: &Schema, name: &str, index: &str, key: &[Expr]) -> Result<Visibility, &'static str> {
    let table = schema.table(name).ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    let sync = table.sync.as_ref().ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    if sync.horizon.is_some() { return Err("the target has a horizon"); }
    let rule = table.rules.read.device_audience_rule();
    // Public subset sync has no whole-table absence proof. Its group coverage
    // is a separate decision; do not infer it merely from the public read rule.
    if rule.is_uniformly_readable() && sync.audience != crate::schema::Audience::Public { return Err("the key's synced group is not established"); }
    let index = table.index(index).ok_or("the key does not pin readability")?;
    let pinned = crate::small_map::collect(index.components.iter().zip(key).filter_map(|(part, expression)| part.column().map(|column| (column, expression))));
    let visibility = prefix_visibility(name, table, &rule, &pinned);
    if !visibility.uniform { return Err("the key does not pin readability"); }
    Ok(visibility)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — simplify self-witnesses, then pre-check only the leading conjuncts whose fields the scope pins, in evaluation order.
