        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn term(term: &RuleTerm, viewer: &str, old: Option<&Value>, next: &Value, probe: &mut dyn Probe, missing: &mut bool) -> Result<Value, crate::error::RuntimeError> {
    Ok(match term {
        RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) => { probe.field(field, false); next.field(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null) },
        RuleTerm::OldField(field) => { probe.field(field, true); old.and_then(|old| old.field(field).ok()).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null) },
        RuleTerm::Reference { column, old: previous, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, old: previous, .. } => {
            probe.field(column, *previous);
            let row = if *previous { old } else { Some(next) };
            let mut value = row.and_then(|r| r.field(column).ok()).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            for (table, field) in term.references() {
                match probe.reference(table, &value, field)? {
                    Some(next) => value = next,
                    None => { *missing = true; return Ok(Value::Null); }
                }
            }
            value
        },
        RuleTerm::Viewer => Value::Id(viewer.to_owned()),
        RuleTerm::Literal(value) => value.clone(),
    })
}

/// The schema-derived probe allowance, independent of short-circuit answers.
pub(crate) fn rule_probes(rule: &RuleExpr) -> u64 {
    let hops = |terms: &[&RuleTerm]| terms.iter().map(|t| t.references().count() as u64).sum::<u64>();
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => hops(&[a,b]),
        RuleExpr::Exists { key, equalities, .. } => 1 + key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)| t)).map(|t| hops(&[t])).sum::<u64>(),
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — created facts with extra equalities take the metered exists path when the fact holds.
        RuleExpr::Created { key, equalities, .. } => u64::from(!equalities.is_empty()) + key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)| t)).map(|t| hops(&[t])).sum::<u64>(),
        RuleExpr::And(terms) | RuleExpr::Or(terms) => terms.iter().map(rule_probes).fold(0, u64::saturating_add),
        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => rule_probes(inner),
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public { .. } => 0,
    }
}

/// A rule's probe sites, as `(table, index)`: what a rule reads besides the
/// row, for live subscriptions and the partition audience.
pub fn probe_sites(rule: &RuleExpr, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
        for (table, _) in t.references() { out.push((table.into(), "byId".into())); }
    }
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { term(a,out); term(b,out); }
        RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
            out.push((table.clone(), index.clone()));
            for t in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)| t)) { term(t,out); }
        }
        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| probe_sites(item, out)),
        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => probe_sites(inner, out),
        _ => {}
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod lifetime_tests {
    use super::*;
    struct Witness { present: bool, until: Option<i64>, expiry: Option<i64>, visited: Vec<String> }
    impl Probe for Witness {
        fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { std::mem::replace(&mut self.expiry, expiry) }
        fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, _: &str, _: &[Value], _: &[(String,Value)]) -> Result<bool, crate::RuntimeError> {
            self.visited.push(table.into());
            if table == "early" { self.expiry = self.until; Ok(self.present) } else { Ok(true) }
        }
        fn created(&mut self, _: &str, _: &str, _: &[Value]) -> bool { true }
    }
    #[test]
    fn branch_selection_uses_verdicts_and_structure_including_created_tests() {
        let exists = |name: &str| RuleExpr::Exists { table: name.into(), index: "byId".into(), key: vec![], provenance: crate::ir::KeyProvenance::Const, equalities: vec![] };
        let created = RuleExpr::Created { table: "early".into(), index: "byId".into(), key: vec![], provenance: crate::ir::KeyProvenance::Const,
            equalities: vec![("kind".into(), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(1)))] };
        for early in [exists("early"), created] {
            for until in [None, Some(2000)] {
                for (rule, present, verdict, visits) in [
                    (RuleExpr::Or(vec![early.clone(), exists("stable")]), true, true, vec!["early", "stable"]),
                    (RuleExpr::And(vec![early.clone(), exists("stable")]), false, false, vec!["early"]),
                    (RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Allow, early.clone()]), true, true, vec![]),
                    (RuleExpr::And(vec![RuleExpr::Deny, early.clone()]), true, false, vec![]),
                ] {
                    let mut probe = Witness { present, until, expiry: None, visited: vec![] };
                    assert_eq!(evaluate(&rule, "alice", None, &Value::Null, true, &mut probe).unwrap(), verdict);
                    assert_eq!(probe.visited, visits);
                    assert_eq!(probe.expiry, None, "stable authority or denial exports no deadline");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Obligation {
    pub path: String,
    pub status: Status,
    pub reason: String,
    pub repair: Option<String>,
}
impl Obligation {
    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
        };
        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    }
}

pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
        let admission = (|| {
            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
            }
            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
            }
            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
        })();
        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
        }
    }
    out
}

fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
        match t {
            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    })
}

    20	fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
    21	pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool)->Result<(),String>{
    22	    let mut record=Record{fingerprint:fingerprint(project,options)?,complete,attempted:campaign.attempted,sequences:campaign.sequences,coverage_gaps:campaign.unsupported.len(),violations:campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).cloned().collect()};
    23	    if let Some(prior)=matching(project,options){
    24	        let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    25	        record.violations=prior.violations.into_iter().chain(record.violations).filter(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&v.invariant_site,&v.viewer,&v.operation,&v.effect,&v.reachability)).unwrap())).collect();
    26	    }
    27	    let path=path(project);std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    28	    let temp=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    29	    let result=std::fs::write(&temp,serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temp,&path));
    30	    if result.is_err(){let _=std::fs::remove_file(temp);}result.map_err(|e|e.to_string())
    31	}
    32	pub(super) fn matching(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Option<Record>{
    33	    let path=path(project);if std::fs::metadata(&path).ok()?.len()>64*1024*1024{return None;}
    34	    let record:Record=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()?;
    35	    (record.fingerprint==fingerprint(project,options).ok()?).then_some(record)
    36	}

//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];
mod paired;
mod timed;
mod histories;
mod assets;
mod column_reads;
mod stability;
mod generated;
pub use generated::fixture as generated_fixture;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, GeneratedFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
impl Reachability {
    pub fn is_reachable(&self)->bool {matches!(self,Self::AuthoredFixtureTrace|Self::GeneratedFixtureTrace)}
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {#[serde(skip)] pub history_limit:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub generated_fixture:bool,#[serde(skip)] pub work_remaining:Option<usize>,#[serde(skip)] pub deadline:Option<std::time::Instant>,pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
impl Campaign {
    pub fn bounded(milliseconds:u64)->Self {Self{work_remaining:Some(512),deadline:Some(std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_millis(milliseconds)),..Self::default()}}
    pub(super) fn reserve(&mut self,cost:usize)->bool {
        if self.exhausted(){return false;}
        if let Some(remaining)=&mut self.work_remaining {
            if *remaining<cost{*remaining=0;self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return false;}
            *remaining-=cost;
        }true
    }
    pub(super) fn exhausted(&mut self)->bool {
        if self.work_remaining==Some(0){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return true;}
        if self.deadline.is_some_and(|deadline|std::time::Instant::now()>=deadline){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration time budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());true}else{false}
    }
}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
        assert_eq!(r.invariants[0].counterexample.as_ref().unwrap().reachability,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace);
    }
}
#[test]
fn generated_memberships_refer_to_valid_resource_and_distinct_owners(){
    let b=bound("table teams:\n  title: text 1..80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable members:\n  team: teams\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('admin','member')\n  unique team, principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\n");
    let mut c=Campaign::default();let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    let teams=fixture.store().rows("teams").unwrap();let members=fixture.store().rows("members").unwrap();
    assert!(teams.len()>=2&&members.len()>=2,"{c:?}");
    assert_ne!(members[0].field("principal"),members[1].field("principal"));
    assert!(members.iter().all(|m|teams.iter().any(|t|t.field("id")==m.field("team"))));
}
#[test]
fn update_only_column_intent_does_not_refute_authorized_creation(){
    let b=bound("table tickets:\n  price: int\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert tickets {price: 1}\ninvariant tickets.price update only viewer = 'service:operator'\n");
    assert_eq!(b.invariants[0].column.as_deref(),Some("price"));
    let mut c=Campaign::default();let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
}
#[test]
fn check_exhaustion_is_explicit_and_retains_no_proof_assumptions(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant notes.body read only .owner = viewer\nquery notes():\n  return notes first 100 by .id\n");
    let r=security::analyze(&b);assert!(r.invariants.iter().all(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved));
    let c=challenges::run_selected_after(&b,None,Campaign::bounded(0));
    assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("time budget exhausted")));assert!(c.violations.is_empty());
    let mut r=security::analyze(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&c);assert!(!r.refuted());
}
#[test]
fn board_advice_names_the_unscoped_branch_but_not_resource_scoped_roles(){
    let source=format!("{ROLES}table reports:\n  owner: principal\n  rating: enum('good','bad')\n  scan: image <=5000 ?\n  read <- .owner = viewer or (exists roles[viewer, role = 'volunteer'] and .rating = 'bad')\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\n");
    let b=bound(&source);assert!(security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));
    let b=bound(&source.replace(" and .rating = 'bad'"," and .owner = viewer and .rating = 'bad'"));
    assert!(!security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));

//! Validate the complete command before any project, store or host work.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — admission, every synopsis and flag help share this command table.
use std::sync::OnceLock;
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Flag { name: String, value: String, #[serde(default)] retired: bool }
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Command { verb: String, positional: String, flags: Vec<Flag>, #[serde(skip)] allowed: String }
fn commands() -> &'static [Command] {
    static TABLE: OnceLock<Vec<Command>> = OnceLock::new();
    TABLE.get_or_init(|| {
        let mut commands: Vec<Command> = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/bin/cli-commands.json")).expect("embedded command table");
        for command in &mut commands { command.allowed = command.flags.iter().map(|flag|flag.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "); }
        commands
    })
}
fn command(verb: &str) -> Option<&'static Command> { commands().iter().find(|command|command.verb==verb) }
pub(crate) fn allowed(verb: &str) -> &'static str { command(verb).map_or("", |command| command.allowed.as_str()) }

pub fn validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let verb = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    // Pipe-owned private commands have positional protocols, not public flags.
    if matches!(verb, "--internal-witness-process" | "--internal-worker-watchdog") { return Ok(()); }
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "--" { break; }
        if arg == "-h" { continue; }
        let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            if arg.starts_with('-') && !arg[1..].starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an invalid flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no {arg}") });
            }
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = flag.split_once('=').map_or((flag, None), |(name, value)| (name, Some(value)));
        if name != "help" && !allowed(verb).split_whitespace().any(|known| known == name) {
            if verb == "rollback" && name == "dry-run" { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback preview is --check; use rollback <dir> [--to N|latest] --check".into()); }
            // Witness arguments may contain bearer material even in a flag name.
            return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an unknown flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no --{name}") });
        }
        if switch(name) {
            if inline.is_some() { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} takes no value")); }
        } else if inline.is_none() {
            // Retired spellings route to their existing no-work explanation,
            // even with a missing value; new unknown flags still refuse here.
            if retired(verb,name) {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            let Some(value) = iter.next() else { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value")); };
            // Search text is literal, including leading dashes and punctuation.
            if value.starts_with("--") && !matches!(name, "find" | "section") {
                return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value"));
            }
        }

//! Positive campaign evidence only; never a cached security proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — exact source/fixture/executable binding.
use super::*;
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
#[derive(serde::Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub(super) struct Record{pub fingerprint:String,pub complete:bool,pub attempted:usize,pub sequences:usize,pub coverage_gaps:usize,pub violations:Vec<security::challenges::Counterexample>}
fn fingerprint(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Result<String,String>{
    let mut hash=Sha256::new();hash.update(b"SEC4-test-record-v1/full-default/NOW=1800000000000");
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    for (name,text) in super::super::proof_source::sources(project,options,flag)?{hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((text.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(text);}
    let binary=std::env::current_exe().and_then(std::fs::read).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;hash.update(Sha256::digest(binary));
    let path=options.flags.get("fixture").map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(||{
        let saved=options.flags.get(flag).map(std::path::Path::new).map(|p|if p.is_dir(){p.join("fixtures")}else if p.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q"){p.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures")}else{p.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",p.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy()))});
        saved.filter(|p|p.is_dir()).unwrap_or_else(||project.root.join("fixtures"))
    });
    if path.is_dir(){let mut files=std::fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;files.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
        for f in files{if !f.file_type().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file(){continue;}let name=f.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();let bytes=std::fs::read(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((bytes.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(bytes);}
    }Ok(format!("{:x}",hash.finalize()))
}
fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool)->Result<(),String>{
    let mut record=Record{fingerprint:fingerprint(project,options)?,complete,attempted:campaign.attempted,sequences:campaign.sequences,coverage_gaps:campaign.unsupported.len(),violations:campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).cloned().collect()};
    if let Some(prior)=matching(project,options){
        let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        record.violations=prior.violations.into_iter().chain(record.violations).filter(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&v.invariant_site,&v.viewer,&v.operation,&v.effect,&v.reachability)).unwrap())).collect();
    }
    let path=path(project);std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let temp=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    let result=std::fs::write(&temp,serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temp,&path));
    if result.is_err(){let _=std::fs::remove_file(temp);}result.map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
pub(super) fn matching(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Option<Record>{
    let path=path(project);if std::fs::metadata(&path).ok()?.len()>64*1024*1024{return None;}
    let record:Record=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()?;
    (record.fingerprint==fingerprint(project,options).ok()?).then_some(record)
}

    23	    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;

   101	    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Campaign::default();
   102	    let populated_fixture=bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|table|engine.store().rows(table).is_ok_and(|rows|!rows.is_empty()));
   103	    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&populated_fixture{
   104	        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
   105	            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
   106	            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
   107	        }
   108	    }else{
   109	        snapback4_lang::security::challenges::generated_campaign(&bound,&mut security_campaign,true);
   110	    }
   111	    let security_campaign=snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run_selected_after(&bound,None,security_campaign);
   112	    let mut security_campaign=security_campaign;
   113	    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
   114	    if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {if let Err(why)=security::remember(&project,options,&security_campaign,security_http_ok){eprintln!("security: campaign record unavailable: {why}; check cannot reuse these refutations");}}
   115	    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
    31	    }
    32	    names.sort();
    33	    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    34	    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    35	    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    36	    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    37	    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    38	    for name in names {
    39	        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
    40	        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
    41	        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
    42	        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
    43	            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
    44	                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
    45	                continue;
    46	            }
    47	            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
    48	            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
    49	                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
    50	                continue;
    51	            }
    52	            unknown.push(name);
    53	            continue;
    54	        };
    55	        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
}

#[test]
fn all_escalation_locations_survive_the_default_five_note_cap(){
 let app=App::new();let mut source=String::new();
 for n in 0..7{source.push_str(&format!("table roles{n}:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('manager','guard')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable secrets{n}:\n  read <- exists roles{n}[viewer,role = 'manager']\n  online only\nmutation register{n}(role: enum('manager','guard')):\n  return insert roles{n} {{principal: viewer,role}}\n"));}
 app.write("snapback/schema.q",&source);let check=app.run(&["check"]);let body=text(&check);assert!(check.status.success(),"{body}");for n in 0..7{assert!(body.contains(&format!("register{n}: roles{n}")),"{body}");}assert!(body.contains("'manager'"),"{body}");
}
